# Changelog

_Generated by `dotnet run --project Build.fsproj -- Changelog` (Phase 262) from the committed public-API
baselines under `api-baselines/`. Do not edit by hand: regenerate, and `-- Changelog --check` refuses a stale
file._

Each release lists the public-surface movement since the release before it, read from the baseline diff the
approval gate (Phase 175 / 618) decides every commit with. Counts are complete; each list is capped per
package — **Removed** and **Changed** generously, because they are what stops compiling, **Added** tightly —
and a truncated list ends with the `git diff` that shows the rest. A new or withdrawn package is one line
with its member count. A member both removed and added with the
same name in one release is reported once, as a signature change. Under this repository's SemVer-on-0.x
policy a `breaking` release is a MINOR bump and an `additive` one a PATCH; from 1.0.0 the ordinary table
applies. Where a release ships a migration note under `docs/migrations/`, it is linked from the section.
